## `AFG-0810-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Role:** Aggregate Crushing Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Aggregate Gradation & Crushing Value Index Testing

- **Key tolerance:** Jaw crusher gap: ±1.0 mm; CSS: ±2.0 mm; Sieve shaker time: 10 ± 0.5 min; Scale accuracy: ±0.01 kg; Load cell accuracy: ±0.5% of reading.
